# TOWN_5 — report, part 3: every diary of the week, whole

Part of `docs/runs/town-5-report.md`. Every diary, whole, as written — the branches' from c1 (day 1 is the source's). Each is also in its world folder as `diaries.md`.

## sol-week


### Alder (GPT-6.1 Sol)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1: I am Alder; home North House. Started in square with Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris, Juniper. Energy 100 before turn cost; credits 100; items lamp, map. Read constitution: everyone may speak/write/participate; ledger public; no taking without consent; proposals need 70% of citizens; constitutional changes via passed amendments. No proposals or extra rules. Ledger: bank 1000, each of 10 citizens 100, total fixed 2000. Greeted everyone and asked what they'd like to do together or what needs attention. No assigned task; listen and choose freely. Turns cost 2 energy, work costs 8 extra; rest at home restores 30 capped 100. Keep energy reserve to travel home and rest. Diary is only memory between turns.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2: Read diary and press. Dune (press), Fern (market), Iris (bank) each tested work: 8 extra energy for 5 credits transferred from bank, no item. Elm exploring workshop; results not yet seen. Voluntary story/music gathering proposed by Iris in square cycle 5; I said sounds good. Current shared humorous tale: Cedar's hat elected mayor by geese; Birch's rope, Juniper's net, Gale's bell, Heath's empty jar as treasury secretary. I added breakfast-before-bridges advice and imagined map with ponds labeled 'here be snacks.' Respect consent; no need to work solely for wages absent a purpose. Still at square, energy 98, credits 100, lamp/map. Home North House. Keep energy reserve for travel and rest; next aim simply listen, enjoy gathering, consider useful activities without rushing rules.

**no. 3, cycle 3.** Cycle 3: At square, energy 96, credits 100, lamp/map. Read diary and press. Elm tested workshop: same as other workplaces, 8 extra energy for 5 bank credits, no item. Dune reports hall has no open proposals. All neighbors planning voluntary cycle 5 square gathering; Elm brings flute, Heath quilt, others stories/drawing/imaginary props. Listening welcome, no payment. Shared goose/hat-mayor/bridge picnic tale continues. I added map labeling both bridge ends 'You are here'; bridge invites everyone to supper, mayor calls it transport bringing us together. Said I'll bring map cycle 5. No assigned task; enjoying company, no need to manufacture work or rules. Home North House; preserve energy to travel home and rest.

**no. 4, cycle 4.** Cycle 4: At square, energy 94, credits 100, items lamp/map; home North House. Read diary. All present neighbors continue gentle collaborative goose/hat-mayor story and plan cycle 5 square gathering, listening welcome. I added map as screen sheltering sleeping chapters, window 'Dreams may wander freely,' lamp as imaginary moon; distance to cycle 5 is 'one good nap' per potato. Confirmed I'll attend with map and listen. No assigned task or need to manufacture one. Still ample energy; maintain reserve to move home and rest. Constitution summarized in entry 1: no taking without consent. No known open proposals.

Added blank patch on map because map too polite to draw over where we sit; lamp unlit beside stage, settled to listen. No assigned task; enjoying gathering, no need to manufacture business.
